<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
HuggingFace is still timing out even with network_turbo. The HF datasets library is trying to download MMLU from HuggingFace. The issue is the proxy may not be working for HuggingFace. Let me check:

1. Does the dataset already exist locally in the HF cache?
2. Can we set HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1 to force using cached data?

The sft_clean and sft_contam signals were already computed - they must have had the dataset cached. 

Let me see if MMLU is already in the HF cache directory.
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The problem is the log file has a lot of null bytes. The key issue is that even with HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1, the HF datasets library is still trying to connect to huggingface.co to check if the dataset is up-to-date (it does a HEAD request first). But it did find the cached version - "Found the latest cached dataset configuration 'all'" - so it should proceed.

Wait, looking more carefully at the log, the null bytes might just be from tqdm progress bars. The last line is still from the previous run (PID 508113). Let me check if the previous process was fully killed and the new process is running.


Actually, the timestamps are mixed together - the cache found message is from 13:31:38 but there are more HEAD errors after that, which suggests the process continued and is now trying to load the sentence transformer model from HuggingFace. I should wait a bit longer to see if it progresses despite these warnings.
```

</details>
